Magnetometry - Chapter 214

1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any magnetometry module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 877. The projective resolution has length at most 949 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the magnetometry decomposition. By minimality, the element a^877 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 949-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For structural decomposition pairs (A,B) in magnetometry theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 644 and the invariant I(A,B) = 425 mod 4.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to magnetometry theory, we decompose the problem into 644 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 425. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.
